  107:              * It is important to remember that a backing `ArrayBuffer` can cover a range

  112:              * ```js
  113:              * import { Buffer } from 'node:buffer';
  114:              *
  115:              * const arrA = Uint8Array.from([0x63, 0x64, 0x65, 0x66]); // 4 elements
  116:              * const arrB = new Uint8Array(arrA.buffer, 1, 2); // 2 elements
  117:              * console.log(arrA.buffer === arrB.buffer); // true
  118:              *
  119:              * const buf = Buffer.from(arrB.buffer);
  120:              * console.log(buf);
  121:              * // Prints: <Buffer 63 64 65 66>
  122:              * ```
  123:              * @since v5.10.0
  124:              * @param arrayBuffer An `ArrayBuffer`, `SharedArrayBuffer`, for example the
  125:              * `.buffer` property of a `TypedArray`.
  126:              * @param byteOffset Index of first byte to expose. **Default:** `0`.
  127:              * @param length Number of bytes to expose. **Default:**

   73: function pickMinMaxFromDocs(docs) {
   74:   let minYmd = null;
   75:   let maxYmd = null;
   76: 
   77:   for (const doc of docs || []) {
   78:     const d = doc?.data ? doc.data() : doc || {};
   79: 
   80:     const y =
   81:       d?.ymd ||
   82:       normalizeToYMD(
   83:         d?.date ?? d?.data ?? d?.dt ?? d?.draw_date ?? d?.close_date
   84:       );

  210: /**
  211:  * =========================
  212:  * ✅ Regras de negócio: UF x lottery_key
  213:  * =========================
  214:  *
  215:  * IMPORTANTE:
  216:  * - lottery_key identifica a loteria/fonte (ex.: PT_RIO)
  217:  * - uf deve ser o estado (ex.: RJ), pois o frontend consulta por UF ("RJ")
  218:  *
  219:  * Se uf ficar "PT_RIO", o bounds por UF quebra.
  220:  */
  221: const RJ_STATE_CODE = "RJ";
  222: const RJ_LOTTERY_KEY = "PT_RIO";
  223: 
  224: const FEDERAL_STATE_CODE = "BR";
  225: const FEDERAL_LOTTERY_KEY = "FEDERAL";
  226: 
  227: const LOOK_STATE_CODE = "GO";
  228: const LOOK_LOTTERY_KEY = "LOOK";
  229: 
  230: const NACIONAL_STATE_CODE = "BR";
  231: const NACIONAL_LOTTERY_KEY = "NACIONAL";
  232: 
  233: function resolveUfFromLotteryKey(lotteryKey) {
  234:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  235:   if (!lk) return null;
  236:   if (lk === RJ_LOTTERY_KEY) return RJ_STATE_CODE;
  237:   if (lk === FEDERAL_LOTTERY_KEY) return FEDERAL_STATE_CODE;
  238:   if (lk === LOOK_LOTTERY_KEY) return LOOK_STATE_CODE;

   10: function isYmdStrict(s) {
   11:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
   12:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
   13:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
   14:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   15:   return (
   16:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
   17:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
   18:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
   19:   );
   20: }

    3:

    5:  * DateRangeControl (Premium, sem libs) — CONTROLADO DE VERDADE

    7:  * Regra:

  463: function isIndexErrorMessage(err) {
  464:   const msg = String(err?.message || err || "").toLowerCase();
  465:   return (
  466:     msg.includes("failed-precondition") ||
  467:     msg.includes("failed_precondition") ||

  475: function clampRangeToBounds(next, minDate, maxDate) {
  476:   const f = next?.from;
  477:   const t = next?.to;
  478: 
  479:   if (!isISODate(f) || !isISODate(t)) return null;
  480:   if (!minDate || !maxDate) return null;
  481:   if (!isISODate(minDate) || !isISODate(maxDate)) return null;
  482: 
  483:   let from = f < minDate ? minDate : f;
  484:   let to = t > maxDate ? maxDate : t;
  485: 
  486:   if (from > to) to = from;
  487:   return { from, to };
  488: }

  196: function lotteryLabelFromKey(key) {
  197:   const s = safeStr(key).toUpperCase();
  198:   if (s === "PT_RIO") return "RIO";
  199:   if (s === "FEDERAL") return "FEDERAL";
  200:   if (s.length === 2) return s;
  201:   const parts = s.split("_");
  202:   return parts[parts.length - 1] || s;
  203: }

  208: 
  209: function clampYmd(ymd, minDate, maxDate) {
  210:   const d = normalizeToYMD(ymd);
  211:   if (!isYMD(d)) return null;
  212: 
  213:   let out = d;
  214: 
  215:   if (isYMD(minDate) && out < minDate) out = minDate;
  216:   if (isYMD(maxDate) && out > maxDate) out = maxDate;
  217: 
  218:   return out;
  219: }
  220: 
  221: function normalizeRangeWithBounds(fromIn, toIn, minDate, maxDate) {
  222:   let from = clampYmd(fromIn, minDate, maxDate);
  223:   let to = clampYmd(toIn, minDate, maxDate);
  224: 
  225:   const fallback = clampYmd(todayYMDLocal(), minDate, maxDate) || maxDate || minDate || todayYMDLocal();

--- ocorrência linha 221 ---
  209: function clampYmd(ymd, minDate, maxDate) {
  210:   const d = normalizeToYMD(ymd);
  211:   if (!isYMD(d)) return null;
  212: 
  213:   let out = d;
  214: 
  215:   if (isYMD(minDate) && out < minDate) out = minDate;
  216:   if (isYMD(maxDate) && out > maxDate) out = maxDate;
  217: 
  218:   return out;
  219: }
  220: 
  221: function normalizeRangeWithBounds(fromIn, toIn, minDate, maxDate) {
  222:   let from = clampYmd(fromIn, minDate, maxDate);
  223:   let to = clampYmd(toIn, minDate, maxDate);
  224: 
  225:   const fallback = clampYmd(todayYMDLocal(), minDate, maxDate) || maxDate || minDate || todayYMDLocal();
  226: 
  227:   if (!from) from = fallback;
  228:   if (!to) to = from;
  229: 
  230:   if (from > to) [from, to] = [to, from];
  231: 
  232:   return { from, to };
  233: }
